is called without explicit options:\n\n| Validator | Trigger Condition | Behavior |\n|-----------|-------------------|----------|\n| **nbf check** | `ignoreNotBefore` not explicitly set to `true` `exp` claim value |\n| **iat/maxAge check** | Only if `maxAge` option is provided as a number | Rejects if `iat + maxAge` < current time |\n\nWhen invoked as `createVerifier({ key, allowedIss:
